[śrī gaṇeśāya namaḥ ]
śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitaḥ
kṛtyakalpataruḥ
brahmacārikāṇḍam
[prārthanā]
[viṣṇuḥ ]
devaḥ sphuranmahimatattadanantamatsya-
kūrmādijanmakathitādbhutavaibhavo yaḥ |
brahmāṇḍabudabudamayo sphuradaṃśaleśaḥ
māyāmapohatu sa bodhamahodadhiḥ || 1 ||
[śivaḥ ]
jāne yasya lalāṭalocanaśikhā lekhā'pi cāndrī vibho
raṃmāśī mamayaṃ (?) jagajjanayituṃ bījāya saṃjāyate |
tasyormivyapayogiyogijanatāhṛtpadmasadmasthita-
sphāradhvāntaharaṃ harasya daharasthānaṃ mahāmo mahaḥ || 2 ||
[manuḥ ]
suhṛyaḥ sukṛtāpuṇyakṣīranīravivecanaḥ |
manurmānasamāviśya rājahaṃsaḥ punātu vaḥ || 3 ||
[[page 117-to-117]]
kṛtyakalpatarau
[rājñaḥ praśaṃsā ]
śrīmanmānavavāsavassa vijayī govindacandro'sti yaḥ
krīḍātarjita gauḍayarjita[bha] yastambhībhava[tpārthivaḥ ] |
sarvorvīndrajayapratiśrutiviyat saṅkocanāśaṅkinā
yenātmāpi jitastataśśamabhṛtā manye kimanye pare || 4 ||
eṣa jñānaparākramaikavasati govindacandraḥ paraṃ
kandarpasya ca vairiṇāṃ ca viśikhaiḥ rakto'bhitaḥ śobhate |
māyāmapyavanīśamaṇḍalamapi khacchandamucchindatā
yenātmā kṛtinā dinaiḥ katipayairadvaitamāropitaḥ || 5 ||
kośān geheṣvamuñcan pathi karituragān bāndhavānardhamārge
durge ntaḥpurāṇi pratiravacakitāḥ parvatebhyo nivṛttāḥ |
yasyodyoge dravante'samasamarasamārambhagambhīra bherī-
bhāṃkārākīrṇakarṇajvara bharataralaprekṣitāḥ śātraveśāḥ || 6 ||
sa kila sakalahaptakṣatranakṣatralakṣmī-
haraṇa kiraṇamālī kasya na syānnamasyaḥ |
asamasamarasampallampaṭaḥ śaurya bhājā-
mavadhiravadhi yuddhe yena hammīravīraḥ || 7 ||
[granthakartuḥ praśaṃsā ]
vidyāvallivilāsa bhūruhavaro vīrasya viprottamaḥ
śrīlakṣmīdhara ityacintyamahimā tasyāsti mantrīśvaraḥ |
bhūbhartuḥ padacakravākamithunakrīḍārthamāsūtritā
yenānekanarendraratnamukuṭajyotirmayī vāhinī || 8 ||
hutvā lakṣamarātibhūpatiśiraḥ padmāni śauryānale
pṛthvīṃ sādhayatā samudravasanāṃ śrīdevakāśīpateḥ |
[[page 118-to-118]]
brahmacārikāṇḍe prārthanādi
etenottamasādhakena kati na krūrāśayāḥ prāpitāḥ
saṃgrāmavrata naiṣṭhikena vilayaṃ vidyārthinā pārthivāḥ || 9 ||
[granthasya praśaṃsā ]
tenāyaṃ vedamūlaḥ smṛtinicayamayaskandhavān yaḥ purāṇa-
vyūhavyutpannaśākhaḥ prakaraṇanikaroddaṇḍakāṇḍapracaṇḍaḥ |
unmīladvākyapatraḥ sphuradativiśadajñānaramyaḥ prasūnaḥ
śrīmaddharmārthakāmāmṛtamayaphaladastanyate kalpavṛkṣaḥ || 10 ||
paurāṇīreva vāṇīḥ kacidakṛta kṛtau kāpi bhūyaḥ smṛtīnāṃ
gopālastadvayasyaḥ svakṛtiviracanaṃ vākyarūpeṇa cakre |
śrautasmārtādisārairvibudhajanamanohāri kāriṣyate'yaṃ
mīmāṃsottaṃsitārthairapṛthuratha kathātītarandhaḥ prabandhaḥ || 11 ||
yasyotthitau sthitimatī na mahārṇave śrīḥ
nidrāṃ dadhāti yadadhaḥ kila kāmadhenuḥ |
tasyātaniṣyati ratiṃ vibudhadvijānāṃ
ānandanaḥ kimu na kalpataroḥ prarohaḥ || 12 ||
kā cintaiva mahārṇavānusaraṇe kā kāmadhenusṭahā
kṣudrāḥ kasya parisphuranti hṛdaye te ratnamālādayaḥ |
śrīlakṣmīdharabuddhivaibhavasudhāsekaprabhāvādayaṃ
yenātraiva jagattrayopakṛtaye kalpadrumaḥ kalpate || 13 ||
ekārtheṣvekamekaṃ kvacidaparamapi svīkṛtaṃ kāryayogāt
nyastaṃ vijñānamūlaṃ pracaradapi parityaktamajñānamūlam |
śiṣṭaissamyaggṛhītaṃ vacanamabhihitaṃ spaṣṭitaṃ cāsphuṭārtha
yatrāpāro virodhaḥ sphurati viracitā tena tatra vyavasthā ||

kṛtyakalpatarau
lakṣmīdhareṇa munimukhyavacassamudraṃ
nyāyādriṇā kila vilojya samuddhṛto'yam |
kāṇḍaiścaturdaśabhiradbhutaparvabandhaiḥ
vāñchocitaṃ phalati kalpataru rna kasya || 15 ||
[brahmacārikāṇḍopakrama ślokaḥ pratijñā ca ]
' vitrastāḥ vimukhāḥ kṣatendriyasukhāḥ kṣmātalpanidrālavo
bhikṣāvṛttijuṣastvacā paricitāḥ kaupīnamātrāmbarāḥ |
nītāḥ kāśyadhipasya yena ripavaste brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ
kāṇḍe sa prathamāśramaṃ prathamataḥ prastauti lakṣmīdharaḥ ||16||
kṛtyakalpatarāvādye kāṇḍe'smin brahmacāriṇaḥ |
svarūpaphalato mānanimittaḥ dharmanirṇayaḥ ||
paribhāṣātha saṃskāraḥ tathopanayana kriyā |
vidhirācamanasyātha nimittaṃ śaucameva ca ||
saṃdhyāvidhyagnikārye ca abhivādopasaṃgrahau |
mānyā ācāryagurvādisaṃjñāḥ tanmānanakramaḥ ||
gurvācāryānuvṛttistu putrabhāryānuvartanam |
indriyāṇāṃ nigrahaśca niyamā brahmacāriṇām ||
adhyāpyo'dhyāpanavidhiḥ svādhyāyadivasetarau |
adhyeyaṃ caturāśramyavyavasthā naiṣṭhikakriyā ||
ācārya dakṣiṇāsnāne iti dvāviṃśati kramāt |
parvāṇyabhinirūpyante vistṛtāni vibhāgaśaḥ ||
1 mūlamātṛkāyāṃ viśālā : ( ? ) vimukhāḥ iti.
2 ' kṣatendriyamukhāḥ' iti mātṛkāyām |

so'dharmaḥ |

kṛtyakalpatarau
āpane rakṣitavyaṃ tu dayaiṣā parikīrtitā ||
bāhye vādhyātmake caiva duḥkhe cotpādite kacit |
na kupyati na vā hanti sā kṣamā parikīrtitā ||
na guṇān guṇino hanti stauti mandaguṇānapi |
nānyadoṣeṣu ramate sānasūyā prakīrtitā ||
abhakṣyaparihāraśca saṃsargaścāpyaninditaiḥ |
svadharme ca vyavasthānaṃ śaucametatprakīrtitam ||
śarīraṃ pīḍyate yena suśubhenāpi karmaṇā |
atyantaṃ tanna kartavyamanāyāsaḥ sa ucyate ||
praśastācaraṇaṃ nityamapraśastavisarjanam |
etaddhi maṅgalaṃ proktamṛṣibhistatvadarśibhiḥ ||
stokādapi hi dātavyamadīnenāntarātmanā |

1 ' deśadharmaḥ' kautukamaṅgalādiḥ | ' jātidharmaḥ ' ābhīrādīnāṃ dantarāgabhāvādi: |

śāstrayoreva nyāyamūlaṃ prāmāṇikam | 'adṛṣṭārthe' dharmaśāstrārthe vikalpa:, 'vyavasthāsaṃbhave '
viśeṣavyavasthitisaṃbhave'sati sati tu vyavasthaiva| 'smṛtiśāstravikalpaḥ ' dharmaśāstrayoḥ virodhe

smṛtyādīnāṃ samuccayaḥ sūcitaḥ | "

1
tadaṅgasyākriyāyāṃ tu nāvṛttirna ca tatkriyā ||
'pravṛttaṃ' prārabdham | 'anyathā' viparītam | tatkarma-
karaṇe satyeva kramādyanyatvena 'yato' yasmātpadā[rthā]dārabhya
tatkarmā'nyathā jātaṃ ta[ta] evārabhya punaryathoktaprakāreṇa kuryā -
dityarthaḥ | 'samāpte' iti, samāpte tu karmaṇi anyathākaraṇa-
jñāne sati, yatpadārthānyathākaraṇaṃ jñā[taṃ] tameva padārtha punaḥ
kuryāt | na punastaduttarānapi padārthān | etadanyathākaraṇa-
viṣayam | pradhānasyetyādi tvakaraṇaviṣayam | pradhānasya karmaṇaḥ

'karakaṃ' kamaṇḍaluḥ* |
[iti kṛtyakalpatarau brahmacārikāṇḍe gurudakṣiṇāstrāne |]
[ iti mahārājādhirāja - śrīgovinda candradeva mahāsandhivigrahika
śrībhagṛhṛdayadharātmaja - śrīlakṣmīdharabhaṭṭasya kṛtau kṛtyakalpatarau
brahmacārikāṇḍam ||]
Brahmacāri Kāṇḍa has been edited from a single manuscript,
the only one so far discovered. It is very defective. Pandit Giridhar-
lāl S'astri, who compared the transcript made for me at Udaipur from
[[page 395-to-395]]
280
kṛtyakalpatagai
[22
the original manuscript has made the following note at the end of the
transcript :
|
makṣikāsthāne makṣiketi niyamamanusṛtyāvalokitam | yataḥ pracīnapratirapyanati
śuddhaiva | kṣantavyam |
1
The concluding passage in the manuscript reads-
karakaṃ' kamaṇḍaluḥ | kuṇḍikā maṇḍalīśakurute | ācāryasya sakāśāt prāpnoti |
asyā vidyācāryasyāpi ya ācāryā te vaṃśyā te'pyasya brahmacāriṇaḥ ācāryāḥ | yāniti
asya brahmacāriṇaḥ paśyataḥ sataḥ yānanyān natvā gururupasaṃgṛhṇīyāt tadā tvevaṃ kāle ete
upasaṃgrāhyāḥ | paiṭhīnasiḥ brahmacaryasyāsamāpteḥ nṛtavedācāryo guruvṛtiṃ samastāṃ putre vartayet |
avidyamāne putre bhrātari vaset ||
These passages obviously form a comment on the Sutras of
Apastamba on p. 264 infra, which stood with only a portion of the
comment. They have accordingly been shifted to p. 264 and printed
after the above Sutras. The passage from Paiţhinasi also relates to
the same topic, and has been printed after the above comments on
āpastamba's Sutras on p. 264. It is evidently a case of the shifting
of folia in the original from which the Udaipur Manuscript was copied.
'
As now printed, the last words of Brahmacāri Kāṇḍa are
karakaṃ' kamaṇḍaluḥ || The usual colophons marking the termination of the
parva and Kāṇḍa are missing in the manuscript. The work appears
to be complete, as it stands, though it is possible that one or two
citations are lost. Lakṣmidhara deals with Snātakavratāni, on
pp. 274-302 of Grhastha Kānda (G.O.S., CI, 1944).
